OBJECT Report 8053055 CMC Sales - Invoice
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=30/10/14;
    Heure=00:00:00;
    Liste versions=CMC02.02.00;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    Permissions=TableData 7190=rimd;
    CaptionML=[ENU=Sales - Invoice;
               FRA=Ventes : Facture];
    TopMargin=0;
    BottomMargin=400;
    LeftMargin=500;
    OnInitReport=BEGIN
                   GLSetup.GET;
                   CompanyInfo.GET;
                   SalesSetup.GET;

                   CASE SalesSetup."Logo Position on Documents" OF
                     SalesSetup."Logo Position on Documents"::"No Logo":;
                     SalesSetup."Logo Position on Documents"::Left:
                       BEGIN
                         CompanyInfo.CALCFIELDS(Picture);
                       END;
                     SalesSetup."Logo Position on Documents"::Center:
                       BEGIN
                         CompanyInfo1.GET;
                         CompanyInfo1.CALCFIELDS(Picture);
                       END;
                     SalesSetup."Logo Position on Documents"::Right:
                       BEGIN
                         CompanyInfo2.GET;
                         CompanyInfo2.CALCFIELDS(Picture);
                       END;
                   END;
                 END;

    OnPreReport=BEGIN
                  IF NOT CurrReport.USEREQUESTFORM THEN
                    InitLogInteraction;
                END;

    Orientation=Portrait;
    PaperSize=A4 210 x 297 mm;
    HorzGrid=210;
  }
  DATAITEMS
  {
    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemTable=Table112;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(No.);
        NewPagePerRecord=Oui;
        ReqFilterHeadingML=[ENU=Posted Sales Invoice;
                            FRA=Facture vente enregistr'e];
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        //--> 20140203-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-285-FHS01
                        recNavAgriSetup_G.GET();
                        //<-- 20140203-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-285-FHS01
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           CurrReport.LANGUAGE := Language.GetLanguageID("Language Code");

                           IF RespCenter.GET("Responsibility Center") THEN BEGIN
                             FormatAddr.RespCenter(CompanyAddr,RespCenter);
                             CompanyInfo."Phone No." := RespCenter."Phone No.";
                             CompanyInfo."Fax No." := RespCenter."Fax No.";
                           END ELSE BEGIN
                             FormatAddr.Company(CompanyAddr,CompanyInfo);
                           END;
                            CLEAR(Capital_G);
                            IF STRPOS(CompanyInfo."Stock Capital",'0')>0 THEN
                               Capital_G :=STRSUBSTNO('Capital : %1 ',CompanyInfo."Stock Capital");

                            CLEAR(AgrementNo_G);
                            IF CompanyInfo."Agrement No."<>'' THEN
                            AgrementNo_G :=STRSUBSTNO('%1 %2',Text1011,CompanyInfo."Agrement No.");
                           CompanyInfo.CALCFIELDS("Certificate Logo",Picture);

                           PostedDocDim1.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"Sales Invoice Header");
                           PostedDocDim1.SETRANGE("Document No.","Sales Invoice Header"."No.");

                           IF "Order No." = '' THEN
                             OrderNoText := ''
                           ELSE
                             OrderNoText := FIELDCAPTION("Order No.");
                           IF "Salesperson Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
                             SalesPurchPerson.INIT;
                             SalesPersonText := '';
                           END ELSE BEGIN
                             SalesPurchPerson.GET("Salesperson Code");
                             SalesPersonText := Text000;
                           END;
                           IF "Your Reference" = '' THEN
                             ReferenceText := ''
                           ELSE
                             ReferenceText := FIELDCAPTION("Your Reference");
                           IF "VAT Registration No." = '' THEN
                             VATNoText := ''
                           ELSE
                             VATNoText := FIELDCAPTION("VAT Registration No.");
                           IF "Currency Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
                             GLSetup.TESTFIELD("LCY Code");
                             TotalText := STRSUBSTNO(Text001,GLSetup."LCY Code");
                             TotalInclVATText := STRSUBSTNO(Text002,GLSetup."LCY Code");
                             TotalExclVATText := STRSUBSTNO(Text006,GLSetup."LCY Code");
                           END ELSE BEGIN
                             TotalText := STRSUBSTNO(Text001,"Currency Code");
                             TotalInclVATText := STRSUBSTNO(Text002,"Currency Code");
                             TotalExclVATText := STRSUBSTNO(Text006,"Currency Code");
                           END;
                           FormatAddr.SalesInvBillTo(CustAddr,"Sales Invoice Header");
                           IF NOT Cust.GET("Bill-to Customer No.") THEN
                             CLEAR(Cust);

                           IF "Payment Terms Code" = '' THEN
                             PaymentTerms.INIT
                           ELSE BEGIN
                             PaymentTerms.GET("Payment Terms Code");
                             PaymentTerms.TranslateDescription(PaymentTerms,"Language Code");
                           END;
                           IF "Shipment Method Code" = '' THEN
                             ShipmentMethod.INIT
                           ELSE BEGIN
                             ShipmentMethod.GET("Shipment Method Code");
                             ShipmentMethod.TranslateDescription(ShipmentMethod,"Language Code");
                           END;
                           FormatAddr.SalesInvShipTo(ShipToAddr,"Sales Invoice Header");
                           ShowShippingAddr := "Sell-to Customer No." <> "Bill-to Customer No.";
                           FOR i := 1 TO ARRAYLEN(ShipToAddr) DO
                             IF ShipToAddr[i] <> CustAddr[i] THEN
                               ShowShippingAddr := TRUE;

                           IF LogInteraction THEN
                             IF NOT CurrReport.PREVIEW THEN BEGIN
                               IF "Bill-to Contact No." <> '' THEN
                                 SegManagement.LogDocument(
                                   4,"No.",0,0,DATABASE::Contact,"Bill-to Contact No.","Salesperson Code",
                                   "Campaign No.","Posting Description",'')
                               ELSE
                                 SegManagement.LogDocument(
                                   4,"No.",0,0,DATABASE::Customer,"Bill-to Customer No.","Salesperson Code",
                                   "Campaign No.","Posting Description",'');
                             END;
                                IF NOT PayReg_G.GET("Payment Method Code") THEN
                                      PayReg_G.INIT;
                                IF NOT BankAccount_G.GET(CompanyInfo."Default Bank Account No.") THEN
                                      BankAccount_G.INIT;
                                CLEAR(TxtModeReglement_G);
                                IF "Payment Method Code"= 'CHEQUE' THEN
                                    TxtModeReglement_G  := STRSUBSTNO(Text2030,CompanyInfo.Name,CompanyInfo.Address+' '+CompanyInfo."Address 2"
                                          ,CompanyInfo."Post Code",CompanyInfo.City);
                              CLEAR(TxtTitre);
                              IF "Prepayment Invoice" THEN
                                 TxtTitre := Text1022
                               ELSE
                                 TxtTitre := Text1021;
                         END;

        ReqFilterFields=No.,Sell-to Customer No.,No. Printed;
      }
      SECTIONS
      {
      }
       }
    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=1;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro);
        NewPagePerRecord=Oui;
        DataItemVarName=CopyLoop;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        NoOfLoops := ABS(NoOfCopies) + Cust."Invoice Copies" + 1;
                        IF NoOfLoops <= 0 THEN
                          NoOfLoops := 1;
                        CopyText := '';
                        SETRANGE(Num'ro,1,NoOfLoops);
                        IF ISSERVICETIER THEN
                          OutputNo := 1;
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           IF Num'ro > 1 THEN BEGIN
                             CopyText := Text003;
                             IF ISSERVICETIER THEN
                               OutputNo += 1;
                           END;
                           CurrReport.PAGENO := 1;

                           IF ISSERVICETIER THEN BEGIN
                             TotalSubTotal := 0;
                             TotalInvoiceDiscountAmount := 0;
                             TotalAmount := 0;
                             TotalAmountVAT := 0;
                             TotalAmountInclVAT := 0;
                             TotalPaymentDiscountOnVAT := 0;
                           END

                           ;
                           CLEAR(CotisationTVA_G);
                           CLEAR(Costisation_G);
                           CLEAR(CotisationT_G);
                           CLEAR(CotisationTot_G);
                           Escompte_G:=0;
                         END;

        OnPostDataItem=BEGIN
                         IF NOT CurrReport.PREVIEW THEN
                           SalesInvCountPrinted.RUN("Sales Invoice Header");
                       END;

      }
      SECTIONS
      {
      }
       }

      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro)
                          WHERE(Num'ro=FILTER(1..));
        DataItemVarName=DimensionLoop1;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        IF NOT ShowInternalInfo THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           IF Num'ro = 1 THEN BEGIN
                             IF NOT PostedDocDim1.FIND('-') THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;
                           END ELSE
                             IF NOT Continue THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;

                           CLEAR(DimText);
                           Continue := FALSE;
                           REPEAT
                             OldDimText := DimText;
                             IF DimText = '' THEN
                               DimText := STRSUBSTNO(
                                 '%1 %2',PostedDocDim1."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim1."Dimension Value Code")
                             ELSE
                               DimText :=
                                 STRSUBSTNO(
                                   '%1, %2 %3',DimText,
                                   PostedDocDim1."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim1."Dimension Value Code");
                             IF STRLEN(DimText) > MAXSTRLEN(OldDimText) THEN BEGIN
                               DimText := OldDimText;
                               Continue := TRUE;
                               EXIT;
                             END;
                           UNTIL (PostedDocDim1.NEXT = 0);
                         END;

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table113;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Document No.,Line No.);
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        VATAmountLine.DELETEALL;
                        SalesShipmentBuffer.RESET;
                        SalesShipmentBuffer.DELETEALL;
                        FirstValueEntryNo := 0;
                        CLEAR(TxtAmntHT);
                        TxtAmntHT := Text2001;
                        IF "Sales Invoice Header"."Prepayment Invoice" THEN
                            TxtAmntHT := Text2002;
                        MoreLines := FIND('+');
                        WHILE MoreLines AND (Description = '') AND ("No." = '') AND (Quantity = 0) AND (Amount = 0) DO
                          MoreLines := NEXT(-1) <> 0;
                        IF NOT MoreLines THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;
                        SETRANGE("Line No.",0,"Line No.");
                        CurrReport.CREATETOTALS("Line Amount",Amount,"Amount Including VAT","Inv. Discount Amount");

                        IF ISSERVICETIER THEN BEGIN
                          GetTotalLineAmount := 0;
                          GetTotalInvDiscAmount := 0;
                          GetTotalAmount := 0;
                          GetTotalAmountIncVAT := 0;
                        END;
                        FOR i:=1 TO 4 DO BEGIN
                          CLEAR(Costisation_G[i]);
                          CLEAR(CotisationT_G[i]);
                          CLEAR(CotisationB_G[i]);
                          CLEAR(CotisationTot_G[i]);
                         END;
                          j:=0;

                             TitreAcopmte := 'Acompte';
                             ValAcompte :=0;
                         IF "Sales Invoice Header"."Prepayment Invoice" THEN  BEGIN
                             TitreAcopmte := '% Acompte';
                             ShowORderCaption := TRUE;
                             END;
                          TotalAmountRedev :=0;
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=VAR
                           InsertCVO@1000000000 : Boolean;
                         BEGIN
                           PostedShipmentDate := 0D;
                           IF Quantity <> 0 THEN
                             PostedShipmentDate := FindPostedShipmentDate;

                           IF (Type = Type::"G/L Account") AND (Quantity=(-1)) AND ("System-Created Entry"=TRUE) THEN
                               CurrReport.SKIP;

                           IF (Type = Type::"G/L Account") AND (NOT ShowInternalInfo) THEN
                             "No." := '';

                           VATAmountLine.INIT;
                           VATAmountLine."VAT Identifier" := "VAT Identifier";
                           VATAmountLine."VAT Calculation Type" := "VAT Calculation Type";
                           VATAmountLine."Tax Group Code" := "Tax Group Code";
                           VATAmountLine."VAT %" := "VAT %";
                           VATAmountLine."VAT Base" := Amount;
                           VATAmountLine."Amount Including VAT" := "Amount Including VAT";
                           VATAmountLine."Line Amount" := "Line Amount";
                           IF "Allow Invoice Disc." THEN
                             VATAmountLine."Inv. Disc. Base Amount" := "Line Amount";
                           VATAmountLine."Invoice Discount Amount" := "Inv. Discount Amount";
                           VATAmountLine.InsertLine;

                              IF "RPD Item Attached Line"<>0 THEN
                                 TotalAmountRedev := TotalAmountRedev + Amount;

                           IF ("CVO Item Attached Line"<>0) AND ("Unit Price"<>0) THEN BEGIN
                             InsertCVO:=FALSE;
                             IF j<>0 THEN
                               FOR i:=1 TO j DO
                                IF (CotisationT_G[i]= "Unit Price") AND (Costisation_G[i]='AVOIR CVO SEMENCES') THEN BEGIN
                                 CotisationTot_G[i] := CotisationTot_G[i]+Amount;
                                 CotisationB_G[i] := CotisationB_G[i]+"Quantity (Base)";
                                 InsertCVO:=TRUE;
                                END;
                              IF (j=0) OR (InsertCVO=FALSE) THEN BEGIN
                                 j:=j+1;
                             Costisation_G[j] := 'AVOIR CVO SEMENCES';
                             CotisationT_G[j] := "Unit Price";
                             CotisationB_G[j] := "Quantity (Base)";
                             CotisationTot_G[j] := Amount;
                              END;
                              //CurrReport.SKIP;
                              END;
                           IF ("CVO Corn Item Attached Line"<>0) AND ("Unit Price"<>0) THEN BEGIN
                             InsertCVO:=FALSE;
                             IF j<>0 THEN
                               FOR i:=1 TO j DO
                                IF (CotisationT_G[i]= "Unit Price") AND (Costisation_G[i]='CVO MAIS') THEN BEGIN
                                 CotisationTot_G[i] := CotisationTot_G[i]+Amount;
                                 CotisationB_G[i] := CotisationB_G[i]+"Quantity (Base)";
                                 InsertCVO:=TRUE;
                                END;
                              IF (j=0) OR (InsertCVO=FALSE) THEN BEGIN
                                 j:=j+1;
                             Costisation_G[j] := 'CVO MAIS';
                             CotisationT_G[j] := "Unit Price";
                             CotisationB_G[j] := "Quantity (Base)";
                             CotisationTot_G[j] := Amount;
                              END;
                              //CurrReport.SKIP;
                              END;

                           IF ("Discount Line") AND ("Unit Price"<>0) THEN BEGIN
                             InsertCVO:=FALSE;
                             IF j<>0 THEN
                               FOR i:=1 TO j DO
                                IF (CotisationT_G[i]= "Unit Price") AND (Costisation_G[i]='Escompte') AND (CotisationTVA_G[i]="VAT %") THEN BEGIN
                                 CotisationTot_G[i] := CotisationTot_G[i]+Amount;
                                 CotisationB_G[i] := CotisationB_G[i]+"Quantity (Base)";
                                 Escompte_G:=Escompte_G + "Sales Invoice Line"."Amount Including VAT";// 20120831-wi00-CAR01.07.00-TLM14371-JLT01
                                 InsertCVO:=TRUE;
                                END;
                                IF (j=0) OR (InsertCVO=FALSE) THEN BEGIN
                                  j:=j+1;
                                  Costisation_G[j] := 'Escompte';
                                  CotisationT_G[j] := "Unit Price";
                                  CotisationB_G[j] := "Quantity (Base)";
                                  CotisationTot_G[j] := Amount;
                                  CotisationTVA_G[j] := "VAT %";
                                  Escompte_G:=Escompte_G +"Sales Invoice Line"."Amount Including VAT";// 20120831-wi00-CAR01.07.00-TLM14371-JLT01
                                END;
                              //CurrReport.SKIP;
                              END;

                           IF ISSERVICETIER THEN BEGIN
                             TotalSubTotal += "Line Amount";
                             TotalInvoiceDiscountAmount -= "Inv. Discount Amount";
                             TotalAmount += Amount;
                             TotalAmountVAT += "Amount Including VAT" - Amount;
                             TotalAmountInclVAT += "Amount Including VAT";
                             TotalPaymentDiscountOnVAT += -("Line Amount" - "Inv. Discount Amount" - "Amount Including VAT");
                           END;
                              CLEAR(ShipmentInvoiced);
                              ShipmentInvoiced.RESET;
                           IF IncludeShptNo THEN BEGIN
                             ShipmentInvoiced.RESET;
                             ShipmentInvoiced.SETRANGE("Invoice No.","Sales Invoice Line"."Document No.");
                             ShipmentInvoiced.SETRANGE("Invoice Line No.","Sales Invoice Line"."Line No.");
                           END;
                         END;

        DataItemLinkReference=Sales Invoice Header;
        DataItemLink=Document No.=FIELD(No.);
      }

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table113;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Document No.,Line No.);
        DataItemVarName=SalesInvoiceLine;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                          IF "Sales Invoice Header"."Prepayment Invoice" THEN
                            CurrReport.BREAK;
                        MoreLines := FIND('+');
                        WHILE MoreLines AND (Description = '') AND ("No." = '') AND (Quantity = 0) AND (Amount = 0) DO
                          MoreLines := NEXT(-1) <> 0;
                        IF NOT MoreLines THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;
                        SETRANGE("Line No.",0,"Line No.");
                        CurrReport.CREATETOTALS("Line Amount",Amount,"Amount Including VAT","Inv. Discount Amount");
                          InsertCVO_G := FALSE;
                         FOR i:=1 TO 20 DO BEGIN
                            CLEAR(FamillePrdouit_G[i]);
                            CLEAR(Valeurfamille_G[i]);
                            END;
                            NbreFamille_G :=0;

                        TotalLineAmount_G:=0;
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=VAR
                           Item_L@1000000004 : Record 27;
                           Manufactrer_L@1000000003 : Record 5720;
                           ShippedHeader_L@1000000002 : Record 110;
                           Contact_L@1000000001 : Record 5050;
                           Cust_L@1000000000 : Record 18;
                           CreateFamProd_G@1000000006 : Boolean;
                           CMCFamily_L@1000000007 : Record 8052986;
                           ItemVariant_L@1000000008 : Record 5401;
                           CUItemTrackingMngt_L@8053000 : Codeunit 6500;
                           InvoiceRowID_L@8053001 : Text[250];
                         BEGIN
                           HideLine_G:=FALSE;
                           IF ("CVO Item Attached Line"<>0) AND ("Unit Price"<>0) THEN BEGIN
                              //CurrReport.SKIP;
                              HideLine_G:=TRUE;
                              "Line Amount" :=0;
                           END;
                           IF ("CVO Corn Item Attached Line"<>0) AND ("Unit Price"<>0) THEN BEGIN
                              //CurrReport.SKIP;
                              HideLine_G:=TRUE ;
                              "Line Amount" :=0;
                           END;

                           IF ("Discount Line") AND ("Unit Price"<>0) THEN BEGIN
                            //CurrReport.SKIP;
                            HideLine_G:=TRUE;
                            "Line Amount" :=0;
                           END;

                           //--> 20140203-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-285-FHS01
                           RpdAmount_G := 0;
                           //<-- 20140203-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-285-FHS01

                           IF "RPD Item Attached Line"<>0 THEN BEGIN
                             //--> 20140203-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-285-FHS01
                             //BEFORE
                             {
                             "Line Amount" :=0;
                             IF  (InsertCVO_G=FALSE) THEN BEGIN
                               j:=j+1;
                               Costisation_G[j] := 'RPD';
                               //CotisationT_G[j] := "Unit Price";
                               //CotisationB_G[j] := "Quantity (Base)";
                               CotisationTot_G[j] := Amount;
                               CotisationTVA_G[j] := "VAT %";
                               InsertCVO_G := TRUE;
                             END ELSE BEGIN
                               // Costisation_G[j] := 'REDEVANCE POLLUTIONS DIFFUSES';
                               //CotisationT_G[j] := "Unit Price";
                               //CotisationB_G[j] := "Quantity (Base)";
                               CotisationTot_G[j] := CotisationTot_G[j] +Amount;
                             END;
                             }
                             //AFTER
                             RpdAmount_G := "Sales Invoice Line"."Line Amount";
                             //<-- 20140203-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-285-FHS01
                           END;

                           IF (Type = Type::"G/L Account") AND (Quantity=(-1)) AND ("System-Created Entry"=TRUE) THEN
                             CurrReport.SKIP;

                           CLEAR(TxtFabricantOrd_G);
                           CLEAR(Item_L);
                           CLEAR(Manufactrer_L);
                           CLEAR(ShippedHeader_L);
                           CLEAR(CreateFamProd_G);
                           CLEAR(CMCFamily_L);
                           IF Type=Type::Item THEN BEGIN
                             IF Item_L.GET("No.") THEN BEGIN
                               //--> 20140203-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-285-FHS01
                               IF recNavAgriSetup_G."Posting RPD Deduction" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                                 RpdAmount_G := Item_L.CalcUnitPollutionTaxe("Posting Date", "Location Code", "Variant Code") * "Sales Invoice Line".Quantity
                           ;
                               END;
                               //<-- 20140203-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-285-FHS01
                               IF Item_L."Manufacturer Code"<>'' THEN
                                 IF Manufactrer_L.GET(Item_L."Manufacturer Code") THEN
                                   IF Manufactrer_L.Name > '' THEN // 20120712-wi00-CAR01.07.00-JLT01
                                     TxtFabricantOrd_G := STRSUBSTNO(Text1013,Manufactrer_L.Name);

                               IF Item_L."Family Code"<>'' THEN BEGIN
                                 IF CMCFamily_L.GET(Item_L."Family Code") THEN ;
                                 FOR i:=1 TO NbreFamille_G DO
                                   IF FamillePrdouit_G[i]= STRSUBSTNO('%1 %2',CMCFamily_L.Name,"VAT Prod. Posting Group") THEN BEGIN
                                     Valeurfamille_G[i]:= Valeurfamille_G[i] + Amount;
                                     CreateFamProd_G := TRUE;
                                   END;
                                 IF NOT CreateFamProd_G THEN BEGIN
                                   NbreFamille_G := NbreFamille_G + 1;
                                   FamillePrdouit_G[NbreFamille_G]:= STRSUBSTNO('%1 %2',CMCFamily_L.Name,"VAT Prod. Posting Group");
                                   Valeurfamille_G[NbreFamille_G]:=  Amount;
                                 END;
                               END;
                             END;

                             ShipmentInvoiced.RESET;
                             ShipmentInvoiced.SETRANGE("Invoice No.","Document No.");
                             ShipmentInvoiced.SETRANGE("Invoice Line No.","Line No.");
                             IF Item_L."Sub-Family Code"='ACM' THEN BEGIN
                               IF ShipmentInvoiced.FINDFIRST THEN
                                 IF ShippedHeader_L.GET(ShipmentInvoiced."Shipment No.") THEN
                                   IF ShippedHeader_L."Prescription No."<>'' THEN BEGIN
                                     IF TxtFabricantOrd_G='' THEN
                                       TxtFabricantOrd_G := STRSUBSTNO(Text1014,ShippedHeader_L."Prescription No.")
                                     ELSE
                                       TxtFabricantOrd_G := TxtFabricantOrd_G+'          '+STRSUBSTNO(Text1014,ShippedHeader_L."Prescription No.");
                                   END;
                               CLEAR(Cust_L);
                               CLEAR(Contact_L);
                               Cust_L.GET("Sales Invoice Header"."Sell-to Customer No.");
                               IF Cust_L."Veterinarian Contact Code"<>'' THEN
                                 IF Contact_L.GET(Cust_L."Veterinarian Contact Code") THEN BEGIN
                                   IF TxtFabricantOrd_G='' THEN
                                     TxtFabricantOrd_G := STRSUBSTNO(Text1016,Contact_L."Salutation Code",Contact_L.Name,Contact_L."Post Code",
                                         Contact_L.City)
                                   ELSE
                                     TxtFabricantOrd_G := TxtFabricantOrd_G+' - '+STRSUBSTNO(Text1016,Contact_L."Salutation Code",
                                       Contact_L.Name,Contact_L."Post Code", Contact_L.City) ;

                                 END;
                             END;
                           END;

                           IF "Item Charge Pricing Item No."<>'' THEN
                             IF Item_L.GET("Item Charge Pricing Item No.") THEN
                               IF Item_L."Family Code"<>'' THEN BEGIN
                                 IF CMCFamily_L.GET(Item_L."Family Code") THEN ;
                                 FOR i:=1 TO NbreFamille_G DO
                                   IF FamillePrdouit_G[i]= STRSUBSTNO('%1 %2',CMCFamily_L.Name,"VAT Prod. Posting Group") THEN BEGIN
                                     Valeurfamille_G[i]:= Valeurfamille_G[i] + Amount;
                                     CreateFamProd_G := TRUE;
                                   END;
                                 IF NOT CreateFamProd_G THEN BEGIN
                                   NbreFamille_G := NbreFamille_G + 1;
                                   FamillePrdouit_G[NbreFamille_G]:= STRSUBSTNO('%1 %2',CMCFamily_L.Name,"VAT Prod. Posting Group");
                                   Valeurfamille_G[NbreFamille_G]:=  Amount;
                                 END;
                               END;

                           CLEAR(ShipmentInvoiced);
                           ShipmentInvoiced.RESET;
                           IF IncludeShptNo THEN BEGIN
                             ShipmentInvoiced.RESET;
                             ShipmentInvoiced.SETRANGE("Invoice No.","Document No.");
                             ShipmentInvoiced.SETRANGE("Invoice Line No.","Line No.");
                           END;

                           TempItemLedgEntry.DELETEALL;
                           // --> 20130531-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-FBE01
                           //BEFORE
                           //GetItemLedgEntries(TempItemLedgEntry,FALSE);
                           //AFTER
                           InvoiceRowID_L := SalesInvoiceLine.RowID1;
                           CUItemTrackingMngt_L.RetrieveILEFromPostedInv(TempItemLedgEntry,InvoiceRowID_L);
                           // <-- 20130531-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-FBE01
                           IF TempItemLedgEntry.FINDFIRST THEN
                             REPEAT
                               IF TempItemLedgEntry."Lot No."='' THEN
                                 TempItemLedgEntry.DELETE;
                             UNTIL TempItemLedgEntry.NEXT=0;

                           NetunitPrice:=0;
                           IF Quantity<>0 THEN
                             NetunitPrice := "Line Amount"/Quantity;
                           IF Amount=0 THEN
                             CLEAR("VAT Identifier");

                           // 20120712-wi00-CAR01.07.00-JLT01 : impression pour Longbien -->
                           VarianteLongbien1:='';
                           VarianteLongbien2:='';

                           IF STRPOS(COMPANYNAME,'LONGBIEN') > 0 THEN BEGIN
                             IF Type=SalesInvoiceLine.Type::Item THEN BEGIN
                               Item_L.GET(SalesInvoiceLine."No.");
                               SalesInvoiceLine.Description:=Item_L.Description;
                               SalesInvoiceLine."Description 2":='';
                               IF ItemVariant_L.GET("No.",SalesInvoiceLine."Variant Code") THEN BEGIN
                                 VarianteLongbien1:=ItemVariant_L.Description;
                                 VarianteLongbien2:= ItemVariant_L."Description 2";
                               END;
                             END;
                           END;
                           // 20120712-wi00-CAR01.07.00-JLT01 : impression pour Longbien <--


                           //-->20121115-rdl01-CMC02.01.00-PPE
                           SalesLineType_G := SalesInvoiceLine.Type;
                           TotalLineAmount_G += SalesInvoiceLine."Line Amount";
                           //<--20121115-rdl01-CMC02.01.00-PPE
                         END;

        DataItemLink=Document No.=FIELD(No.);

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=4;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro);
        DataItemVarName=KitSalesInvLine;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        IF (SalesInvoiceLine.Type <> SalesInvoiceLine.Type::Item) OR
                           NOT SalesInvoiceLine."Kit Item"

                        THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;
                        IF NOT Item.GET("Sales Invoice Line"."No.") THEN
                          CLEAR(Item);
                        CASE Cust."Components on Invoices" OF
                          Cust."Components on Invoices"::"Do Not Show":
                            CurrReport.BREAK;
                          Cust."Components on Invoices"::" ",Cust."Components on Invoices"::Show:
                            CASE Item."Components on Invoices" OF
                              Item."Components on Invoices"::"Do Not Show":
                                CurrReport.BREAK;
                              Item."Components on Invoices"::" ":
                                IF SalesSetup."Components on Invoices" = SalesSetup."Components on Invoices"::"Do Not Show" THEN
                                  CurrReport.BREAK;
                            END;
                        END;
                        TempKitSalesInvLine.SETRANGE(Type);
                        TempKitSalesInvLine.DELETEALL;
                        KitManagement.GetKitSalesInvLinesToPrint("Sales Invoice Line",TempKitSalesInvLine);
                        TempKitSalesInvLine.SETRANGE(Type,TempKitSalesInvLine.Type::Item);
                        SETRANGE(Num'ro,1,TempKitSalesInvLine.COUNT);
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           IF Num'ro = 1 THEN
                             TempKitSalesInvLine.FINDSET
                           ELSE
                             TempKitSalesInvLine.NEXT;
                         END;

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table113;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Document No.,Line No.);
        DataItemVarName=SalesInvoiceLine2;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                          IF NOT "Sales Invoice Header"."Prepayment Invoice" THEN
                            CurrReport.BREAK;
                        MoreLines := FIND('+');
                        WHILE MoreLines AND (Description = '') AND ("No." = '') AND (Quantity = 0) AND (Amount = 0) DO
                          MoreLines := NEXT(-1) <> 0;
                        IF NOT MoreLines THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;
                        SETRANGE("Line No.",0,"Line No.");
                        CurrReport.CREATETOTALS("Line Amount",Amount,"Amount Including VAT","Inv. Discount Amount");

                        //-->20121115-rdl01-CMC02.01.00-PPE
                        k := 0;

                        //<--20121115-rdl01-CMC02.01.00-PPE
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=VAR
                           CMCFamily_L@1000000000 : Record 8052986;
                           Item_L@1000000001 : Record 27;
                           CreateFamProd_G@1000000002 : Boolean;
                         BEGIN
                             CLEAR(CMCFamily_L);
                             IF Type=Type::Item  THEN
                                IF Item_L.GET("No.") THEN  BEGIN

                                   IF Item_L."Family Code"<>'' THEN BEGIN
                                      IF CMCFamily_L.GET(Item_L."Family Code") THEN ;
                                      FOR i:=1 TO NbreFamille_G DO
                                         IF FamillePrdouit_G[i]= STRSUBSTNO('%1 %2',CMCFamily_L.Name,"VAT Prod. Posting Group") THEN
                                            BEGIN
                                              Valeurfamille_G[i]:= Valeurfamille_G[i] + Amount;
                                              CreateFamProd_G := TRUE;
                                            END;
                                       IF  NOT CreateFamProd_G THEN BEGIN
                                           NbreFamille_G := NbreFamille_G + 1;
                                           FamillePrdouit_G[NbreFamille_G]:= STRSUBSTNO('%1 %2',CMCFamily_L.Name,"VAT Prod. Posting Group");
                                           Valeurfamille_G[NbreFamille_G]:=  Amount;
                                       END;
                                    END;
                                 END;
                               IF "Item Charge Pricing Item No."<>'' THEN
                                 IF Item_L.GET("Item Charge Pricing Item No.") THEN
                                   IF Item_L."Family Code"<>'' THEN BEGIN
                                      IF CMCFamily_L.GET(Item_L."Family Code") THEN ;
                                      FOR i:=1 TO NbreFamille_G DO
                                         IF FamillePrdouit_G[i]= STRSUBSTNO('%1 %2',CMCFamily_L.Name,"VAT Prod. Posting Group") THEN
                                            BEGIN
                                              Valeurfamille_G[i]:= Valeurfamille_G[i] + Amount;
                                              CreateFamProd_G := TRUE;
                                            END;
                                       IF  NOT CreateFamProd_G THEN BEGIN
                                           NbreFamille_G := NbreFamille_G + 1;
                                           FamillePrdouit_G[NbreFamille_G]:= STRSUBSTNO('%1 %2',CMCFamily_L.Name,"VAT Prod. Posting Group");
                                           Valeurfamille_G[NbreFamille_G]:=  Amount;
                                       END;
                                    END;

                           //-->20121115-rdl01-CMC02.01.00-PPE
                           IF ISSERVICETIER THEN
                           BEGIN
                             k += 1;
                             IF k <> 1 THEN
                               ShowORderCaption:=FALSE;
                           END;
                           SalesLineType_G := SalesInvoiceLine2.Type;
                           //<--20121115-rdl01-CMC02.01.00-PPE
                         END;

        DataItemLinkReference=Sales Invoice Header;
        DataItemLink=Document No.=FIELD(No.);
      }

    PROCEDURE FindPostedShipmentDate@6() : Date;
    VAR
      SalesShipmentHeader@1000 : Record 110;
      SalesShipmentBuffer2@1001 : TEMPORARY Record 7190;
    BEGIN
      NextEntryNo := 1;
      IF "Sales Invoice Line"."Shipment No." <> '' THEN
        IF SalesShipmentHeader.GET("Sales Invoice Line"."Shipment No.") THEN
          EXIT(SalesShipmentHeader."Posting Date");

      IF "Sales Invoice Header"."Order No."='' THEN
        EXIT("Sales Invoice Header"."Posting Date");

      CASE "Sales Invoice Line".Type OF
        "Sales Invoice Line".Type::Item:
          GenerateBufferFromValueEntry("Sales Invoice Line");
        "Sales Invoice Line".Type::"G/L Account","Sales Invoice Line".Type::Resource,
        "Sales Invoice Line".Type::"Charge (Item)","Sales Invoice Line".Type::"Fixed Asset":
           GenerateBufferFromShipment("Sales Invoice Line");
        "Sales Invoice Line".Type::" ":
            EXIT(0D);
      END;

      SalesShipmentBuffer.RESET;
      SalesShipmentBuffer.SETRANGE("Document No.","Sales Invoice Line"."Document No.");
      SalesShipmentBuffer.SETRANGE("Line No." ,"Sales Invoice Line"."Line No.");
      IF SalesShipmentBuffer.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
        SalesShipmentBuffer2 := SalesShipmentBuffer;
          IF SalesShipmentBuffer.NEXT = 0 THEN BEGIN
            SalesShipmentBuffer.GET(
              SalesShipmentBuffer2."Document No.",SalesShipmentBuffer2."Line No.",SalesShipmentBuffer2."Entry No.");
            SalesShipmentBuffer.DELETE;
            EXIT(SalesShipmentBuffer2."Posting Date");
          END ;
         SalesShipmentBuffer.CALCSUMS(Quantity);
         IF SalesShipmentBuffer.Quantity <> "Sales Invoice Line".Quantity THEN BEGIN
           SalesShipmentBuffer.DELETEALL;
           EXIT("Sales Invoice Header"."Posting Date");
         END;
      END ELSE
        EXIT("Sales Invoice Header"."Posting Date");
    END;

    PROCEDURE GenerateBufferFromValueEntry@2(SalesInvoiceLine2@1002 : Record 113);
    VAR
      ValueEntry@1000 : Record 5802;
      ItemLedgerEntry@1001 : Record 32;
      TotalQuantity@1003 : Decimal;
      Quantity@1004 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      TotalQuantity := SalesInvoiceLine2."Quantity (Base)";
      ValueEntry.SETCURRENTKEY("Document No.");
      ValueEntry.SETRANGE("Document No.",SalesInvoiceLine2."Document No.");
      ValueEntry.SETRANGE("Posting Date","Sales Invoice Header"."Posting Date");
      ValueEntry.SETRANGE("Item Charge No.",'');
      ValueEntry.SETFILTER("Entry No.",'%1..',FirstValueEntryNo);
      IF ValueEntry.FIND('-') THEN
        REPEAT
          IF ItemLedgerEntry.GET(ValueEntry."Item Ledger Entry No.") THEN BEGIN
            IF SalesInvoiceLine2."Qty. per Unit of Measure" <> 0 THEN
              Quantity := ValueEntry."Invoiced Quantity" / SalesInvoiceLine2."Qty. per Unit of Measure"
            ELSE
              Quantity := ValueEntry."Invoiced Quantity";
            AddBufferEntry(
              SalesInvoiceLine2,
              -Quantity,
              ItemLedgerEntry."Posting Date");
            TotalQuantity := TotalQuantity + ValueEntry."Invoiced Quantity";
          END;
          FirstValueEntryNo := ValueEntry."Entry No." + 1;
        UNTIL (ValueEntry.NEXT = 0) OR (TotalQuantity = 0);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GenerateBufferFromShipment@8(SalesInvoiceLine@1000 : Record 113);
    VAR
      SalesInvoiceHeader@1001 : Record 112;
      SalesInvoiceLine2@1002 : Record 113;
      SalesShipmentHeader@1006 : Record 110;
      SalesShipmentLine@1004 : Record 111;
      TotalQuantity@1003 : Decimal;
      Quantity@1005 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      TotalQuantity := 0;
      SalesInvoiceHeader.SETCURRENTKEY("Order No.");
      SalesInvoiceHeader.SETFILTER("No.",'..%1',"Sales Invoice Header"."No.");
      SalesInvoiceHeader.SETRANGE("Order No.","Sales Invoice Header"."Order No.");
      IF SalesInvoiceHeader.FIND('-') THEN
        REPEAT
          SalesInvoiceLine2.SETRANGE("Document No.",SalesInvoiceHeader."No.");
          SalesInvoiceLine2.SETRANGE("Line No.",SalesInvoiceLine."Line No.");
          SalesInvoiceLine2.SETRANGE(Type,SalesInvoiceLine.Type);
          SalesInvoiceLine2.SETRANGE("No.",SalesInvoiceLine."No.");
          SalesInvoiceLine2.SETRANGE("Unit of Measure Code",SalesInvoiceLine."Unit of Measure Code");
          IF SalesInvoiceLine2.FIND('-') THEN
            REPEAT
              TotalQuantity := TotalQuantity + SalesInvoiceLine2.Quantity;
            UNTIL SalesInvoiceLine2.NEXT = 0;
        UNTIL SalesInvoiceHeader.NEXT = 0;

      SalesShipmentLine.SETCURRENTKEY("Order No.","Order Line No.");
      SalesShipmentLine.SETRANGE("Order No.","Sales Invoice Header"."Order No.");
      SalesShipmentLine.SETRANGE("Order Line No.",SalesInvoiceLine."Line No.");
      SalesShipmentLine.SETRANGE("Line No.",SalesInvoiceLine."Line No.");
      SalesShipmentLine.SETRANGE(Type,SalesInvoiceLine.Type);
      SalesShipmentLine.SETRANGE("No.",SalesInvoiceLine."No.");
      SalesShipmentLine.SETRANGE("Unit of Measure Code",SalesInvoiceLine."Unit of Measure Code");
      SalesShipmentLine.SETFILTER(Quantity,'<>%1',0);

      IF SalesShipmentLine.FIND('-') THEN
        REPEAT
          IF "Sales Invoice Header"."Get Shipment Used" THEN
            CorrectShipment(SalesShipmentLine);
          IF ABS(SalesShipmentLine.Quantity) <= ABS(TotalQuantity - SalesInvoiceLine.Quantity) THEN
            TotalQuantity := TotalQuantity - SalesShipmentLine.Quantity
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF ABS(SalesShipmentLine.Quantity)  > ABS(TotalQuantity) THEN
              SalesShipmentLine.Quantity := TotalQuantity;
            Quantity :=
              SalesShipmentLine.Quantity - (TotalQuantity - SalesInvoiceLine.Quantity);

            TotalQuantity := TotalQuantity - SalesShipmentLine.Quantity;
            SalesInvoiceLine.Quantity := SalesInvoiceLine.Quantity - Quantity;

            IF SalesShipmentHeader.GET(SalesShipmentLine."Document No.") THEN
              BEGIN
                AddBufferEntry(
                  SalesInvoiceLine,
                  Quantity,
                  SalesShipmentHeader."Posting Date");
              END;
          END;
        UNTIL (SalesShipmentLine.NEXT = 0) OR (TotalQuantity = 0);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CorrectShipment@7(VAR SalesShipmentLine@1001 : Record 111);
    VAR
      SalesInvoiceLine@1000 : Record 113;
    BEGIN
      SalesInvoiceLine.SETCURRENTKEY("Shipment No.","Shipment Line No.");
      SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE("Shipment No.",SalesShipmentLine."Document No.");
      SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE("Shipment Line No.",SalesShipmentLine."Line No.");
      IF SalesInvoiceLine.FIND('-') THEN
         REPEAT
            SalesShipmentLine.Quantity := SalesShipmentLine.Quantity - SalesInvoiceLine.Quantity;
         UNTIL SalesInvoiceLine.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    PROCEDURE AddBufferEntry@3(SalesInvoiceLine@1001 : Record 113;QtyOnShipment@1002 : Decimal;PostingDate@1003 : Date);
    BEGIN
      SalesShipmentBuffer.SETRANGE("Document No.",SalesInvoiceLine."Document No.");
      SalesShipmentBuffer.SETRANGE("Line No.",SalesInvoiceLine."Line No.");
      SalesShipmentBuffer.SETRANGE("Posting Date",PostingDate);
      IF SalesShipmentBuffer.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
        SalesShipmentBuffer.Quantity := SalesShipmentBuffer.Quantity + QtyOnShipment;
        SalesShipmentBuffer.MODIFY;
        EXIT;
      END;

      WITH SalesShipmentBuffer DO BEGIN
        "Document No." := SalesInvoiceLine."Document No.";
        "Line No." := SalesInvoiceLine."Line No.";
        "Entry No." := NextEntryNo;
        Type := SalesInvoiceLine.Type;
        "No." := SalesInvoiceLine."No.";
        Quantity := QtyOnShipment;
       "Posting Date" := PostingDate;
        INSERT;
        NextEntryNo := NextEntryNo + 1
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE DocumentCaption@4() : Text[250];
    BEGIN
      IF "Sales Invoice Header"."Prepayment Invoice" THEN
        EXIT(STRSUBSTNO(Text8060991,"Sales Invoice Header"."No."));
      EXIT(STRSUBSTNO(Text8060990,"Sales Invoice Header"."No."))
    END;

    PROCEDURE "<-- 3LI FUNCTIONS 8060990 -->"@8061021();
    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE FindVatPosition@8061022(VatIdentifier_P@8060982 : Code[10]) : Integer;
    VAR
      LastPositionKnown_L@8060983 : Integer;
      VatPositionLine_L@8060984 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // Fonction permettant de connaOtre la position de ligne de TVA dans le tableau r'cap TVA bas de page

      // --> 3LI-20110318-8060990/XXX-CMB6.00.01.01-JDL01
      LastPositionKnown_L := 0;

      FOR VatPositionLine_L := 1 TO ARRAYLEN(BaseTva_G) - 1 DO BEGIN
        IF VATIdentifier_G[VatPositionLine_L] = VatIdentifier_P THEN
          EXIT(VatPositionLine_L);
        IF VATIdentifier_G[VatPositionLine_L] <> '' THEN
          LastPositionKnown_L := VatPositionLine_L;
      END;

      //Si on arrive ici c'est que l'on n'a pas trouv' de position existante donc on prend la derniSre position disponible
      // et on la sort avec un +1 (si d'but traitement 0 + 1 = 1)
      IF LastPositionKnown_L+1 = ARRAYLEN(BaseTva_G) THEN
        ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(Text8060992,"Sales Invoice Header"."No.",ARRAYLEN(BaseTva_G)-1));

      EXIT(LastPositionKnown_L+1);

      // <-- 3LI-20110318-8060990/XXX-CMB6.00.01.01-JDL01
    END;

    PROCEDURE CalcTotalVAT@8060989();
    VAR
      VatPositionLine_L@8060982 : Integer;
      VATLCYExist_L@8060983 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //Permet de calculer la total TVA

      // --> 3LI-20110318-8060990/XXX-CMB6.00.01.01-JDL01
      //Calcul du total
      FOR VatPositionLine_L := 1 TO ARRAYLEN(BaseTva_G) - 1 DO BEGIN
        BaseTva_G[ARRAYLEN(BaseTva_G)] += BaseTva_G[VatPositionLine_L];
        MtTVA_G[ARRAYLEN(BaseTva_G)] += MtTVA_G[VatPositionLine_L];
        BaseTvaLCY_G[ARRAYLEN(BaseTva_G)] += BaseTvaLCY_G[VatPositionLine_L];
        MtTVALCY_G[ARRAYLEN(BaseTva_G)] += MtTVALCY_G[VatPositionLine_L];
      END;


      // Gestion en-t^te et total TVA (d'signation)
      IF (BaseTva_G[ARRAYLEN(BaseTva_G)] = 0) THEN BEGIN
        VATIdentifier_G[ARRAYLEN(BaseTva_G)] := ''
      END ELSE BEGIN
        VATIdentifier_G[ARRAYLEN(BaseTva_G)] := Text8060993;
        DescEntTVA_G[1] := VATAmountLine.FIELDCAPTION("VAT Identifier");
        DescEntTVA_G[3] := VATAmountLine.FIELDCAPTION("VAT %");
        IF (MtTVALCY_G[ARRAYLEN(BaseTva_G)] <> 0) OR (BaseTvaLCY_G[ARRAYLEN(BaseTva_G)] <> 0) THEN BEGIN
          //D'tail TVA LCY demand'
          DescEntTVA_G[2] := STRSUBSTNO('%1 %2',VATAmountLine.FIELDCAPTION("VAT Base"),Text8060996);
          DescEntTVA_G[4] := STRSUBSTNO('%1 %2',VATAmountLine.FIELDCAPTION("VAT Amount"),Text8060996);
          VATLCYExist_L := TRUE;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          DescEntTVA_G[2] := VATAmountLine.FIELDCAPTION("VAT Base");
          DescEntTVA_G[4] := VATAmountLine.FIELDCAPTION("VAT Amount");
          VATLCYExist_L := FALSE;
        END;
        // Gestion des 2 valeurs TVA dans une colonne
        FOR VatPositionLine_L := 1 TO ARRAYLEN(BaseTva_G) DO BEGIN
          IF BaseTva_G[VatPositionLine_L] <> 0 THEN BEGIN
            IF VATLCYExist_L THEN BEGIN
              BaseTvaAsText_G[VatPositionLine_L] := STRSUBSTNO('%1 (%2)',BaseTva_G[VatPositionLine_L],BaseTvaLCY_G[VatPositionLine_L]);
              MtTVAAsText_G[VatPositionLine_L] := STRSUBSTNO('%1 (%2)',MtTVA_G[VatPositionLine_L],MtTVALCY_G[VatPositionLine_L]);
            END ELSE BEGIN
              BaseTvaAsText_G[VatPositionLine_L] := STRSUBSTNO('%1',BaseTva_G[VatPositionLine_L]);
              MtTVAAsText_G[VatPositionLine_L] := STRSUBSTNO('%1',MtTVA_G[VatPositionLine_L]);
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
      // <-- 3LI-20110318-8060990/XXX-CMB6.00.01.01-JDL01
    END;

    PROCEDURE InitVatArray@8060986();
    BEGIN
      // initialise la tableau de TVA

      // --> 3LI-20110318-8060990/XXX-CMB6.00.01.01-JDL01
      FOR VatPositionLine_G :=1 TO ARRAYLEN(BaseTva_G) DO BEGIN
         BaseTva_G[VatPositionLine_G]:=0;
         TxTVA_G[VatPositionLine_G]:=0;
         MtTVA_G[VatPositionLine_G]:=0;
         VATIdentifier_G[VatPositionLine_G]:='';
         BaseTvaLCY_G[VatPositionLine_G]:=0;
         MtTVALCY_G[VatPositionLine_G]:=0;
         BaseTvaAsText_G[VatPositionLine_G]:='';
         MtTVAAsText_G[VatPositionLine_G]:='';
      END;
      DescEntTVA_G[1] := '';
      DescEntTVA_G[2] := '';
      DescEntTVA_G[3] := '';
      DescEntTVA_G[4] := '';

      // <-- 3LI-20110318-8060990/XXX-CMB6.00.01.01-JDL01
    END;

    PROCEDURE ManageTotal@8060982();
    VAR
      CurrencyCode_L@8060982 : Code[20];
    BEGIN
      // --> 3LI-20110318-8060990/XXX-CMB6.00.01.01-JDL01

      // Gestion de la devise
      IF "Sales Invoice Header"."Currency Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
        GLSetup.TESTFIELD("LCY Code");
        CurrencyCode_L := GLSetup."LCY Code";
      END ELSE BEGIN
        CurrencyCode_L := "Sales Invoice Header"."Currency Code";
      END;

      // Gestion du montant remise facture
      InvDiscAmountTxt_G := '';
      InvDiscAmountAmt_G := 0;
      SubTotal_G := 0;
      TotalText := '';
      IF "Sales Invoice Line"."Inv. Discount Amount" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        SubTotal_G := "Sales Invoice Line"."Line Amount";
        TotalText := STRSUBSTNO(Text8060994,CurrencyCode_L);
        InvDiscAmountAmt_G := "Sales Invoice Line"."Inv. Discount Amount";
        InvDiscAmountTxt_G := "Sales Invoice Line".FIELDCAPTION("Inv. Discount Amount");
      END;

      //Gestion des montant HT, TTC, devise ou sans devise
      FirstTotalTxt_G := '';
      FirstTotalAmt_G := 0;
      SecondTotalTxt_G := '';
      SecondTotalAmt_G := 0;
      ThirdTotalTxt_G := '';
      ThirdTotalAmt_G := 0;
      IF "Sales Invoice Line"."Amount Including VAT" = "Sales Invoice Line".Amount THEN BEGIN
        // pas de TVA dans le document
        FirstTotalTxt_G := STRSUBSTNO(Text8060995,CurrencyCode_L);
        FirstTotalAmt_G := "Sales Invoice Line".Amount;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF (NOT "Sales Invoice Header"."Prices Including VAT") THEN BEGIN
          FirstTotalTxt_G := TotalExclVATText;
          FirstTotalAmt_G := "Sales Invoice Line".Amount;
          SecondTotalTxt_G := VATAmountLine.VATAmountText;
          SecondTotalAmt_G := "Sales Invoice Line"."Amount Including VAT" - "Sales Invoice Line".Amount;
          ThirdTotalTxt_G := STRSUBSTNO(Text8060995,CurrencyCode_L);
          ThirdTotalAmt_G := "Sales Invoice Line"."Amount Including VAT";
        END ELSE BEGIN
          FirstTotalTxt_G := STRSUBSTNO(Text8060995,CurrencyCode_L);
          FirstTotalAmt_G := "Sales Invoice Line"."Amount Including VAT";
          SecondTotalTxt_G := VATAmountLine.VATAmountText;
          SecondTotalAmt_G := "Sales Invoice Line"."Amount Including VAT" - "Sales Invoice Line".Amount;
          ThirdTotalTxt_G := TotalExclVATText;
          ThirdTotalAmt_G := "Sales Invoice Line".Amount;

        END;
      END;
      // <-- 3LI-20110318-8060990/XXX-CMB6.00.01.01-JDL01
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      // --> 3LI BEGIN

      20121008-Wi00-CMC02.01.00-DRN01 : NEWOBJ (version CAR Classic)
      20121115-rdl01-CMC02.01.00-PPE : RLD Layout
                                       Add variable SalesLineType_G
      20130423-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-DRN01 : MODOBJ (Modification Sizing Picture et Certificate Logo)
      20130531-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-FBE01 : Correction gestion date d'expiration des Lots
      20140110-WI00-CMC02.02.00-BSA01 : Correction d'affichage du layout
      20140203-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-285-FHS01 : MODCODE (Change affichage pour RPD)
      // <-- 3LI END
    }
    END.
  }

  <Code>Public Function BlankZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value = 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankZeroAndPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNeg(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNegAndZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

REM Reset Page Number:

Shared offset as Integer
Shared newPage as Object
Shared currentgroup1 as Object
Shared currentgroup2 as Object
Shared currentgroup3 as Object

Public Function GetGroupPageNumber(NewPage as Boolean, pagenumber as Integer) as Object
  If NewPage
    offset = pagenumber - 1
  End If
  Return pagenumber - offset
End Function

Public Function IsNewPage(group1 as Object, group2 as Object, group3 as Object) As Boolean
newPage = FALSE
If Not (group1 = currentgroup1)
    newPage = TRUE
    currentgroup1 = group1
    currentgroup2 = group2
    currentgroup3 = group3
     ELSE 
      If Not (group2 = currentgroup2)
        newPage = TRUE 
        currentgroup2 = group2
        currentgroup3 = group3
        ELSE
          If Not (group3 = currentgroup3)
          newPage = TRUE 
          currentgroup3 = group3
         End If
     End If
  End If
Return newPage
End Function

Shared Data1 as Object
Shared Data2 as Object
Shared Data3 as Object
Shared Data4 as Object

Public Function GetData(Num as Integer, Group as integer) as Object
if Group = 1 then
   Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data1),Chr(177))))
End If

if Group = 2 then
   Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data2),Chr(177))))
End If

if Group = 3 then
   Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data3),Chr(177))))
End If

if Group = 4 then
   Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data4),Chr(177))))
End If

End Function

Public Function SetData(NewData as Object,Group as integer)
  If Group = 1 and NewData &lt;&gt; "" Then
      Data1 = NewData
  End If

  If Group = 2 and NewData &lt;&gt; "" Then
      Data2 = NewData
  End If

  If Group = 3 and NewData &lt;&gt; "" Then
      Data3 = NewData
  End If

  If Group = 4 and NewData &lt;&gt; "" Then
      Data4 = NewData
  End If

End Function

Shared PictureData1 as Object
Shared PictureData2 as Object
Shared PictureData3 as Object

Public Function GetPicture(Group as integer) as Object
if Group = 1 then
   Return PictureData1
End If

if Group = 2 then
   Return PictureData2
End If

if Group = 3 then
   Return PictureData3
End If
End Function

  If Group = 2 and NewData &gt; "" Then
      PictureData2 = NewData
  End If

  If Group = 3 and NewData &gt; "" Then
      PictureData3 = NewData
  End If
End Function</Code>
